--- FILE: app\utils\date_parser.py ---
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, date, time

def parse_date_range(text: str):
    today = date.today()

    # Contextual keywords
    if "next month" in text:
        return None, None, "next month"
    if "next week" in text:
        return None, None, "next week"
    if "next" in text:
        return None, None, "next"

    if "previous month" in text:
        return None, None, "previous month"
    if "previous week" in text:
        return None, None, "previous week"
    
    if "previous" in text or "last" in text and "week" not in text and "month" not in text:
         # "previous" or just "last" (without specific timeframe) -> clear context
        return None, None, "previous"

    if "monthly" in text:
        return None, None, "monthly"

    if "today" in text:
        start = datetime.combine(today, time.min)
        end = datetime.combine(today, time.max)
        return start, end, "today"

    if "yesterday" in text:
        y = today - timedelta(days=1)
        start = datetime.combine(y, time.min)
        end = datetime.combine(y, time.max)
        return start, end, "yesterday"

    # Precise "This Week" (Monday to Sunday)
    if "this week" in text or "current week" in text:
        start_day = today - timedelta(days=today.weekday()) # Monday
        start = datetime.combine(start_day, time.min)
        
        # End of week (Sunday)
        end_day = start_day + timedelta(days=6)
        end = datetime.combine(end_day, time.max)
        return start, end, "this week"

    if "last week" in text:
        # Last calendar week (Mon-Sun)
        start_of_this_week = today - timedelta(days=today.weekday())
        start_of_last_week = start_of_this_week - timedelta(days=7)
        end_of_last_week = start_of_last_week + timedelta(days=6)
        
        start = datetime.combine(start_of_last_week, time.min)
        end = datetime.combine(end_of_last_week, time.max)
        return start, end, "last week"

    if "this month" in text:
        start_day = today.replace(day=1)
        start = datetime.combine(start_day, time.min)
        end = datetime.combine(today, time.max)
        return start, end,"this month"

    if "last month" in text:
        first_this_month = today.replace(day=1)
        last_month_end_day = first_this_month - timedelta(days=1)
        last_month_start_day = last_month_end_day.replace(day=1)
        start = datetime.combine(last_month_start_day, time.min)
        end = datetime.combine(last_month_end_day, time.max)
        return start, end,"last month"

    return None, None,"overall"

--- END OF app\utils\date_parser.py ---

--- FILE: app\utils\entity_extractor.py ---
from .date_parser import parse_date_range
from .expense_parser import extract_amount, detect_category

# Common categories in the system
CATEGORIES = ["food", "travel", "rent", "snacks", "shopping", "fuel", "entertainment", "health", "others"]

def extract_entities(text: str) -> dict:
    """
    Extracts amount, category, and date range from the given text.
    Uses the robust expense_parser for better accuracy.
    """
    text_lower = text.lower().strip()
    
    # 1. Extract Amount
    try:
        amount = extract_amount(text_lower)
    except ValueError:
        amount = None
    
    # 2. Extract Category
    # If a specific category exists in text, we prefer the parser's logic
    category = detect_category(text_lower)
    if category == "others":
        # Check if the user mentioned a category name directly
        for cat in CATEGORIES:
            if cat in text_lower:
                category = cat
                break
    
    # 3. Extract Date Range
    start_date, end_date, label = parse_date_range(text_lower)
    
    return {
        "amount": amount,
        "category": category if category != "others" else None,
        "start_date": start_date,
        "end_date": end_date,
        "date_label": label
    }

def get_professional_response(intent: str, entities: dict, success: bool = True, data: dict = None, warning: str = None) -> str:
    """
    Generates a professional, user-friendly response based on intent and result.
    """
    if intent == "add_expense":
        if success:
            msg = f"✅ **Expense Added!**\n\nI've recorded a charge of **₹{entities['amount']}** under **{entities['category'].title()}**. Your spending has been updated."
            if warning:
                msg += f"\n\n⚠️ **Warning**: {warning}"
            return msg
        return "❌ **Oops!** I couldn't quite catch that. Could you please provide the amount and category? (e.g., 'Add 200 for food')"
        
    if intent == "total_expenses":
        total = data.get("total", 0)
        label = entities.get("date_label", "overall")
        category = entities.get("category")
        
        if category:
            return f"📊 **{category.title()} Spending Summary ({label.title()})**\n\nYour total {category} expenses for this period amount to **₹{total}**. Let me know if you need a detailed breakdown!"
        return f"📊 **Spending Summary ({label.title()})**\n\nYour total expenses for this period amount to **₹{total}**. Let me know if you need a detailed breakdown!"

    if intent == "show_expenses":
        expenses = data.get("expenses", [])
        label = entities.get("date_label", "overall")
        category = entities.get("category")
        
        if not expenses:
            cat_msg = f" in {category.title()}" if category else ""
            return f"📂 **No expenses found**{cat_msg} for {label}. You're all clear!"
        
        header = f"📝 **{category.title()} Expense Records ({label.title()})**" if category else f"📝 **Expense Records ({label.title()})**"
        return header

    if intent == "unknown":
        return (
            "👋 **Hi! I'm your Finance Assistant.**\n\n"
            "I can help you manage your money professionally. Try asking:\n"
            "• \"Add 500 for shopping\"\n"
            "• \"What's my total for this month?\"\n"
            "• \"Show my expenses from last week\"\n"
            "• \"Set a budget of 5000 for food\"\n"
            "• \"Show my budget details\""
        )
        
    if intent == "set_budget":
        if success:
            cat_str = f" for **{entities['category'].title()}**" if entities['category'] else ""
            return f"🎯 **Budget Set!**\n\nI've set your monthly spending limit to **₹{entities['amount']}**{cat_str}. I'll help you stay on track!"
        return "❌ **Budget not set.** Please specify an amount. (e.g., 'Set a budget of 5000')"

    if intent == "show_budget":
        budgets = data.get("budgets", [])
        if not budgets:
            return "📋 **No budgets set yet.** You can set one by saying something like 'Set a budget of 5000 for food'."
        
        return "📉 **Your Monthly Budgets**"

    if intent == "add_income":
        if success:
            prefix = "🔄" if data and data.get("updated") else "✅"
            action = "updated" if data and data.get("updated") else "saved"
            return f"{prefix} **Monthly income of ₹{entities['amount']} {action} for {data['month']}/{data['year']}.**"
        return "❌ **Income not saved.** Please specify your monthly income amount. (e.g., 'My salary is 50000')"

    return "🙏 I'm sorry, I didn't quite understand that. Could you try rephrasing? I'm still learning!"

--- END OF app\utils\entity_extractor.py ---

----------------------------------------

--- FILE: app\utils\expense_parser.py ---
import re

def extract_amount(text: str) -> float:
    # Pre-process text to handle 'k' notation and clean numeric strings
    text = text.lower().replace(",", "")
    
    # regex to find numbers potentially followed by 'k'
    # handles cases like 40k, 40 k, 40.5k
    patterns = [
        r"(?:rs|₹|income|salary|earn|paid|spent|cost|for)\s?(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s?(k)?",
        r"(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s?(k)?\s?(?:rs|rupees|income|salary|earnings|per month)",
        r"(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s?(k)?"
    ]
    
    for pattern in patterns:
        match = re.search(pattern, text)
        if match:
            val = float(match.group(1))
            if match.group(2) == 'k':
                val *= 1000
            return val
            
    raise ValueError("Amount not found in text")

def detect_category(text: str) -> str:
    keyword_map = {
        "food": [
            "pizza", "burger", "meal", "lunch", "dinner", "restaurant", "hotel", 
            "swiggy", "zomato", "biryani", "thali", "dosa", "idli", "paneer", 
            "blinkit", "zepto", "bigbasket", "milk", "grocery","food",
        ],
        "snacks": ["chai", "coffee", "chips", "biscuit", "maggi", "momos", "samosa", "chaat", "juice","snacks"],
        "travel": ["bus", "train", "taxi", "uber", "ola", "flight", "rickshaw", "auto", "metro", "rapido", "irctc","travel"],
        "fuel": ["petrol", "diesel", "fuel", "cng", "shell", "iocl", "bpcl","fuel"],
        "entertainment": ["movie", "netflix", "prime", "hotstar", "pvr", "inox", "bookmyshow", "concert", "match","entertainment","games","sports"],
        "health": ["doctor", "medicine", "hospital", "clinic", "pharmacy", "apollo", "1mg", "pharmeasy","health"],
        "shopping": ["shirt", "shoes", "clothes", "amazon", "flipkart", "myntra", "ajio", "meesho", "mall","shopping"],
        "rent": ["rent", "electricity", "water", "gas", "indane", "bijli","rent"]
    }
    
    scores = {cat: 0 for cat in keyword_map}
    text_lower = text.lower()
    
    for category, keywords in keyword_map.items():
        for keyword in keywords:
            if keyword in text_lower:
                scores[category] += 1
                
    max_score = 0
    detected_category = "others"
    
    for category, score in scores.items():
        if score > max_score:
            max_score = score
            detected_category = category
            
    return detected_category

def process_expense(text: str) -> dict:
    amount = extract_amount(text)
    category = detect_category(text)
    return {
        "amount": amount,
        "category": category,
        "message": f"{int(amount) if amount.is_integer() else amount} rs added under {category}"
    }

--- END OF app\utils\expense_parser.py ---

--- FILE: scripts\update_representation.py ---

import os

def generate_representation(root_dir, output_file):
    ignored_dirs = {'venv', '.git', '__pycache__', 'node_modules', '.pytest_cache'}
    ignored_files = {'database.db', 'backend_representation.txt', 'backend_representation.txt.tmp', 'backend_representation.txt.structure'}
    
    with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as out:
        out.write("================================================================================\n")
        out.write("EXPENSE TRACKER BACKEND - FULL REPRESENTATION\n")
        out.write(f"Generated on: {os.popen('date /t').read().strip()} {os.popen('time /t').read().strip()}\n")
        out.write("================================================================================\n\n")
        
        out.write("DIRECTORY STRUCTURE:\n")
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir):
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ignored_dirs]
            level = root.replace(root_dir, '').count(os.sep)
            indent = ' ' * 4 * level
            out.write(f"{indent}{os.path.basename(root)}/\n")
            sub_indent = ' ' * 4 * (level + 1)
            for f in files:
                if f not in ignored_files:
                    out.write(f"{sub_indent}{f}\n")
        
        out.write("\n" + "="*80 + "\n")
        out.write("FILE CONTENTS\n")
        out.write("="*80 + "\n\n")
        
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir):
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ignored_dirs]
            for f in files:
                if f in ignored_files:
                    continue
                
                file_path = os.path.join(root, f)
                rel_path = os.path.relpath(file_path, root_dir)
                
                try:
                    with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace') as f_in:
                        content = f_in.read()
                        out.write(f"--- FILE: {rel_path} ---\n")
                        out.write(content)
                        out.write(f"\n--- END OF {rel_path} ---\n")
                        out.write("\n" + "-"*40 + "\n\n")
                except Exception as e:
                    out.write(f"--- ERROR READING {rel_path}: {str(e)} ---\n\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_representation('.', 'backend_representation.txt')
    print("Backend representation updated successfully.")

--- END OF scripts\update_representation.py ---
